    FROM [standaloneModes] AS [s0]
    LEFT JOIN [standaloneJammings] AS [s1] ON [s0].[StandaloneModeId] = [s1].[StandaloneModeId]
) AS [s2] ON [s].[EmitterId] = [s2].[EmitterId]
ORDER BY [s].[EmitterId], [s2].[StandaloneModeId]
